--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Note (1): The term "Primary" refers to the donor of the most recent 
          common envelope.
Note (2): 
     WD = white dwarf,
    sdB = sub dwarf B star,
    sdO = sub dwarf O star,
     MS = main sequence star,
     NS = neutron star,
     BH = black hole,
     BD = brown dwarf,
     BS = blue straggler, 
     otherwise spectral type.
Note (3): 
    MT = mass transfer,
    CV = cataclysmic variable,
     S = statistically inferred mass (most likely inclination),
   SWD = assumed WD mass,
  SsdB = assumed sdB mass,
    Sq = assumed mass ratio,
   SM2 = assumed companion mass,
   TRI = triple.
Note (4): See file: refs.txt
